\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 3. Final Audit Scope and Preservation Rules\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 This final audit covers whether the Acting revision package is materially responsive to ACTING-SYN-F01\'96F17 and ACTING-AUDIT-R01\'96R16, whether it preserves the current TapeCoach architecture, and whether it is ready to move to Acting Output Specificity / Non-Regression Test Mapping.\
It does not cover live rendered outputs, frontend label helpers, comparison-page label behaviour, renderer/export timestamp count, score recomputation code, schema implementation, production subtype extraction or product implementation.\
The following remain out of scope: backend changes, schema changes, field renames, score-field additions, weighting changes, cap/blocker/verdict changes, role-fit bound changes, pipeline changes, Step 1 / Step 2 rewrites and any change to the stabilised Musical Theatre acting + song flow.\
A return to ACTING-REV would be required only if the revised text introduced a schema/weighting change, failed to contain the spoken voice label risk, collapsed Acting into one generic standard, allowed access needs as deficits, imported external marks/methodologies as weights, or failed to ring-fence live-room capacities. None of those blocking failures are present.\
\pard\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf3 \strokec3 \
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 7. Topic-by-Topic Final Audit Summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\fs28 \cf0 Acting-form specificity\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. The revised baseline now distinguishes monologue, scene/duologue, screen/self-tape, stage, classical/heightened, contemporary and mixed tasks. Residual limitation: comedy and physical/fight remain conditional. No revision loop required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Monologue handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Monologue guidance now requires speech structure, thought shifts, stakes, rhythm, address and text handling. Residual limitation: no live examples supplied. No revision loop required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Scene / duologue / reader handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Reader handling is now process support; performer listening and response remain reportable only where observable. Residual limitation: reader ecology remains under-detailed. No revision loop required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Screen/self-tape task specificity\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Direct-to-camera, off-camera-reader, monologue, ident/slate and read-in-line contexts are separated. Residual limitation: deeper Trinity screen spec was limited. No revision loop required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Stage acting and heightened text handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Heightened text now requires observable sense, rhythm, imagery, thought progression and speech clarity. Residual limitation: stage-versus-screen naturalism evidence remains partly indirect. No revision loop required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Classical versus contemporary text\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass. Classical/heightened and contemporary text are separated and protected from inappropriate cross-application. No revision loop required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Speech / voice delivery versus singing label containment\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass with limitation. Text clearly blocks singing/vocal-performance language where singing is absent. Residual limitation: frontend label behaviour remains unverified. No revision loop required; display QA required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Current vocal-as-speech proxy containment\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Status: pass with limitation. The architectural proxy is contained at wording and display-semantics level without backend renaming. Residual limitation: stored field remains shared. No revision loop required.\

\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 12. Final Release Decision\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\fs24 \cf0 ACTING-FINAL-D01 \'97 Approved with explicit limitations
\f1\b0 \
The revised Acting baseline is materially compliant with ACTING-SYN-F01\'96F17 and materially responsive to ACTING-AUDIT-R01\'96R16 at wording and guardrail level. It makes Acting form-specific inside the current framework, contains spoken voice / vocal-proxy risk without schema change, separates speech audibility from speech performance, codifies self-tape assessability and anti-polish boundaries, ring-fences live-room/process-only capacities, strengthens no-brief claim restraint, protects reader handling as process support, adds accessibility-safe non-deficit language, blocks methodology overreach and prevents external frameworks becoming TapeCoach weights.\
There are no critical blockers requiring a return to ACTING-REV. The remaining issues are limitations rather than revision blockers: the structural vocal/speech proxy remains partly architecture-shaped; frontend labels, comparison labels and renderer/export timestamp behaviour are unverified; live Acting outputs were not supplied; comedy and physical/fight evidence remain limited; and some access-specific operational detail remains broad.\

\f0\b ACTING-FINAL-D02 \'97 Approved for Acting Output Specificity / Non-Regression Test Mapping with explicit limitation set
\f1\b0 \
The next stage can move forward. It must explicitly carry forward tests for label containment, acting-form specificity, screen task handling, assessability versus polish, speech audibility, reader handling, live-room overclaim, accessibility-safe language, no-brief role/world restraint, generic-feedback suppression, conditional comedy/physical/fight handling and timestamp rendering.\
Live Acting output examples are still required before product release sign-off.\
\pard\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf3 \strokec3 \
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 13. Reusable Handoff Pack for ACTING-OUTPUT-SPEC\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\fs28 \cf0 ACTING-FINAL-AUDIT handoff summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 The Acting final audit is complete and the revised Acting baseline is approved with explicit limitations. The revised text is materially compliant with ACTING-SYN-F01\'96F17 and materially responsive to ACTING-AUDIT-R01\'96R16. It does not alter the shared TapeCoach architecture, score fields, Acting weights, caps, blockers, verdict thresholds, role-fit bounds, report schema, backend pipeline or Musical Theatre stabilised flow.\
The strongest accepted changes are: acting-form specificity, spoken voice label containment, screen/self-tape task rules, self-tape assessability and anti-polish boundaries, speech audibility versus speech performance, live-room/process-only claim boundaries, no-brief role/world guardrails, accessibility-safe Acting and speech assessment, reader-as-process support, professional_presentation boundaries, methodology-neutrality, external-framework weighting caution, generic-feedback suppression, and timestamp / next-take specificity.\
Accepted with limitation: ACTING-REV-C02, C04, C10, C13 and C15, mainly because live frontend labels, renderer/export timestamp behaviour and live Acting outputs were not supplied. These limitations do not require another ACTING-REV pass, but they must be tested during ACTING-OUTPUT-SPEC and live QA.\
Residual risks to carry forward are: structural vocal/speech proxy semantics, unverified frontend category labels, unverified comparison-page labels, unverified renderer/export timestamp behaviour, no live Acting output examples, comedy evidence thinness, physical/fight evidence being specialist-course-shaped, methodology-neutral descriptor limits, broad neurodivergence-specific operational detail and unresolved Royal Central source limitation.\
The next stage should convert the accepted baseline into output-specificity rules and adversarial tests. Highest-priority scenarios are spoken-only no-singing label containment, monologue with no brief, reader-mediated scene, direct-to-camera versus off-camera eyeline, poor lighting/framing, poor speech audibility, smartphone/home setup, polished but weak tape, paid/basic reader, response-to-direction overclaim, training-potential overclaim, access-adapted context, speech difference, generic praise, invented role/world, physical/fight overclaim and comedy overclaim.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Compact acceptance summary with ACTING-REV-C IDs\
